# Exact terminal-covariance completion in the original primitive cone

Status: complete joint proof DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max4 analytical
round29, the first of the two rounds authorized after completed baseline28
by TASK.md and master_guidance f7608aa358751108. Numerical, solver,
replay, installation and formal-verifier allowances are all closed.
The only accepted mathematical input is fact04219554e41d9750, Section10,
for the original primitive identity. No other draft is a premise.

The original high-capacity seed and the original exact-profile goal remain
open. The result here is an exact decision of a full-interface affine
fiber: all twenty original coefficient signs and PSD on all five formal
gradients are tested simultaneously, including every singular boundary.
The line is not asserted to cover the whole cone. Its unresolved freedom
and its relation to the existing complete coordinates are explicit below.

## 1. Original interface and the earlier coupled gap

Let h=(h0,h1,h2,h3) be a nonnegative four-step word, P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,
s=P_4>0, and C=1+s. The five standard coordinate columns are e_i,
indices0 through4; e is their sum. Set

    T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
    omega=(-2h0,-2h1,-2h2,-2h3,2s)^T,
    D=diag(h0(2-h0),h1(2-h1),h2(2-h2),h3(2-h3),-s(s+2)).

The original array has Lambda_ii=0 and twenty off-diagonal entries.
Write L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda and M=D+sym(LT), where
sym(A)=(A+A^T)/2. Full feasibility means

    Lambda_ij>=0 for all i!=j, L^T e=omega, M>=0 on R^5.       (1)

The exact identity in accepted04219554e41d9750 is

    V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij = <M,Gram(g0,...,g4)>,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    V_h=2sum_(i<4)h_i(f_i-f4)+sum_(i<4)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                                   -s(s+1)||g4||^2,
    x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i.                                    (2)

Every formal gradient is retained. In particular, a zero step does not
authorize an equality between formal coordinates.

The flow forces e^T M e=0: Te=e+P,
2sum h_i P_i=s^2-sum h_i^2, and

    e^T D e=-s^2-sum h_i^2,
    e^T LT e=omega^T(e+P)=s^2+sum h_i^2.

PSD then implies Me=0, by considering (e+t*z)^T M(e+t*z) for
both signs of t. Conversely, later in this document the flow and Me=0
are imposed before testing PSD; this does not assert that either implies
PSD. The row moments equivalent to Me=0 under the flow are

    sum_j Lambda_ij(P_j-P_i)=2h_i(1+P_i), i<4,
    sum_(j<4)Lambda_4j(s-P_j)=2sC.                           (3)

Define the quadratic column by

    p0=1, p_(i+1)=(1-h_i)p_i.

Then Tp=e, so Le=0 gives

    p^T M p=p^T D p=1-C^2*p4^2=:Qenergy.                    (4)

The last equality telescopes h_i(2-h_i)p_i^2=p_i^2-p_(i+1)^2.
Thus (4) holds for every real array with the flow, without assuming PSD.

For the positive-excess regime, write

    h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,1+d), a,b,c,d>0,
    u=1+a, delta=u^2-2, eta=b/(3+c+d),
    nu=(1+eta)(1-a*eta)/a,
    Erow=a+3+1/a-(u*delta/a)*eta-C^2*eta^2,
    Wrow=max(1+b,1+b*c*d), Krow=max(u,a*Wrow).

When a>sqrt(2)-1, full feasibility forces a<1, a*eta<1,
nu>0 and 0<=Qenergy<1. The complete earlier first-row result is

    u*delta<=Krow*Erow/nu+2*sqrt(Qenergy*Erow/nu),             (5)
    Erow>=nu*[u*delta/
       (sqrt(Qenergy+Krow*u*delta)+sqrt(Qenergy))]^2>0.       (6)

With the ADDITIONAL hypotheses a*b<=1 and c*d<=1, it gives

    Erow>nu*delta^2/u^2,
    b<(a+3)*(C-a-2)/((a+1)*(C+a+2)).                        (7)

No hypothesis in these bounds is removed. The proof of the earlier
joint draft2948d4cd3660413f is reproduced in Appendix A, not cited as
an accepted fact. In particular its exact accounting identity is

    Erow=nu*(M00+a*(Lambda20+Lambda30+Lambda40))
         +(1+eta)^2*M11
         +(1+eta)*eta*((2+c+d)*Lambda12+(1+d)*Lambda13).      (8)

The new completion below preserves EVERY term on the right of (8),
the exact first-row/quadratic cross term, and the endpoint principal
block. Thus (5)--(8) remain simultaneous conditions, rather than a
replacement for either the original signs or the full PSD condition.

## 2. Direct terminal identities and the unpriced covariance

Put v=h3>0 and B=P3=s-v. Write y_j=Lambda_4j for j<4,
n=M44, and delta_v=2C-(C+1)*v. The terminal diagonal is

    n=sum_(j<4)y_j-s(s+2).

Using (3) gives the exact nonnegative budget

    v*n+sum_(j=0)^2(P3-P_j)*y_j=s*delta_v.                  (9)

For PSD arrays this includes n>=0 and every y_j>=0, so delta_v>=0.
There is no division by delta_v or n. If delta_v=0 and h0,h1,h2>0,
then n=0 and y0=y1=y2=0. Full PSD then forces the entire terminal
row of M to vanish. The case of zero prefix steps retains the displayed
weights: a vanishing weight cannot be cancelled to deduce y_j=0.

The exact terminal/quadratic covariance is

    2*e4^T M p=(n-s(s+2))*p4-sum_(i<4)Lambda_i4*p_i.        (10)

Indeed Mp=Dp+T^T L^T p/2 and Te4=e4, while
(L^T p)_4=(sum y_j)*p4-sum_(i<4)Lambda_i4*p_i.
The weighted INCOMING terminal sum in (10) is not priced by (9),
which involves OUTGOING terminal coefficients. Its odd quadratic
coefficients can have the opposite sign from its even coefficients.
Replacing that sum by a nonnegative total mass would lose information.

The endpoint cross term is independently

    2*M04=(h0-1)*Lambda40-Lambda04.                        (11)

These formulas use original rows, not any reversal theorem. They show
where the new cross term enters, but alone do not prove that it is free.
The next section exhibits the precise freedom in the actual array.

## 3. A four-edge motion preserving the already coupled energies

Assume w=h2>0 and v=h3>0. Start with ANY real base array Lambda^0
having the original flow and M^0 e=0. It need not be PSD. For a real
parameter theta change only these four original coefficients:

    Lambda23(theta)=Lambda23^0+(1+v/w)*theta,
    Lambda24(theta)=Lambda24^0-theta,
    Lambda32(theta)=Lambda32^0+(v/w)*theta,
    Lambda34(theta)=Lambda34^0+theta.                       (12)

All other off-diagonal coefficients stay exactly as in the base array.
The diagonal is still zero. Define

    v_move=e2-e3,
    w_move=(v/w)*e2-(1+v/w)*e3+e4.

The change of the row Laplacian is exactly

    L(theta)-L^0=theta*v_move*w_move^T.                     (13)

Both e^T v_move and w_move^T e vanish, and

    w_move^T P=(v/w)*(P2-P3)+(P4-P3)=-v+v=0.

Consequently (13) preserves L e=0, L^T e=omega, and Me=0.
This proves every flow and constant-kernel equation, including those at
the nodes touched by the motion.

Let t_move=T^T w_move. Its only nonzero coordinates are

    (t_move)_2=v/w-v,
    (t_move)_3=v-1-v/w,
    (t_move)_4=1.                                         (14)

For i=0,1, the sum of the later coordinates of w_move is zero,
so (t_move)_i=0. Equations (13)--(14) give the full matrix identity

    M(theta)=M^0+theta*J_move,
    J_move=sym(v_move*t_move^T).                           (15)

In particular both ENTIRE rows0 and1 of M are fixed. Also

    (J_move)_44=0, (J_move)_24=1/2, (J_move)_34=-1/2,
    p^T J_move p=(p^T v_move)*(w_move^T T p)=0,
    e4^T J_move p=(p2-p3)/2=:kappa_move.                    (16)

Thus the terminal diagonal and endpoint block, the quadratic energy,
the first-row/quadratic covariance, and the first-row lifted-cut budget
(8) are all fixed. The last claim also follows entry by entry: none of
Lambda20,Lambda30,Lambda40,Lambda12,Lambda13 changes. Every outgoing
terminal coefficient stays fixed, so the complete budget (9) stays fixed.
The second-row/quadratic covariance e1^T M p stays fixed too.

The four-edge motion is not a PSD-order repair: the principal2by2
submatrix of J_move on coordinates2,4 has determinant -1/4. Thus
J_move is indefinite for EVERY positive w,v. Neither sign of theta
can be declared PSD-preserving without the full test below.

If the sixteen unchanged coefficients are nonnegative, the twenty
original signs hold exactly when

    theta_sign_low<=theta<=theta_sign_high,
    theta_sign_low=max(-Lambda23^0/(1+v/w),
                       -w*Lambda32^0/v,-Lambda34^0),
    theta_sign_high=Lambda24^0.                            (17)

No strict positivity is assumed. If this interval is empty the entire
fiber is infeasible. If it is a singleton that is the only sign-allowed
array; an open perturbation must not be inferred on that boundary.

## 4. Full five-dimensional PSD is one covariance interval

Assume in addition p2!=p3, so kappa_move!=0. This holds throughout
the positive-excess regime because p2-p3=a*b*(1+c)>0.

The five columns e,e0,e1,e4,p form a basis of R^5. Indeed a linear
dependence has, at coordinates2 and3, equations alpha+beta*p2=0 and
alpha+beta*p3=0. Subtraction gives beta=0 and then alpha=0; the
remaining three standard-coordinate coefficients vanish. With Me=0,
full PSD on all R^5 is therefore equivalent to PSD of the following
4by4 matrix in the ordered columns e0,e1,e4,p:

    H(theta) = [ A_piv   r_vec      q_vec
                 r_vec^T n         z(theta)
                 q_vec^T z(theta) Qenergy ],               (18)

where

    A_piv=[[M00^0,M01^0],[M01^0,M11^0]],
    r_vec=(M04^0,M14^0)^T,
    q_vec=(e0^T M^0 p,e1^T M^0 p)^T,
    n=M44^0, z(theta)=z_base+kappa_move*theta,
    z_base=e4^T M^0 p.

All entries except z(theta) are fixed by (15)--(16). This is a basis
change on the original formal space, not a quotient that identifies
any gradients. In particular checking (18) tests the WHOLE M(theta),
not merely the principal block on e0,e4,p.

Here is a complete, boundary-safe way to calculate three scalars

    r_cost, q_cost, center_cov.

First require A_piv>=0. Also require r_vec and q_vec to lie in its
column space. When those conditions hold, define the costs by the
following disjoint cases. Write m=M00^0, k=M01^0, j=M11^0,
r_vec=(r0,r1)^T, q_vec=(q0,q1)^T.

* If det(A_piv)>0, use
  r_cost=r_vec^T A_piv^(-1)r_vec,
  q_cost=q_vec^T A_piv^(-1)q_vec, and
  center_cov=r_vec^T A_piv^(-1)q_vec. The inverse denominator is
  strictly positive in this case.
* If det(A_piv)=0 and m>0, impose
  r1=k*r0/m and q1=k*q0/m. Put
  r_cost=r0^2/m, q_cost=q0^2/m, center_cov=r0*q0/m.
* If m=0 and j>0, PSD of A_piv forces k=0. Impose r0=q0=0
  and put r_cost=r1^2/j, q_cost=q1^2/j, center_cov=r1*q1/j.
* If m=j=0, PSD forces k=0. Impose r_vec=q_vec=0 and set
  r_cost=q_cost=center_cov=0.

These cases exhaust PSD2by2 pivots. A failed column-space requirement
means no theta can work. Define the residual energies

    alpha_res=n-r_cost, beta_res=Qenergy-q_cost.            (19)

Full PSD holds if and only if all the pivot/column-space conditions hold,
alpha_res>=0, beta_res>=0, and

    center_cov-sqrt(alpha_res*beta_res)
       <= z_base+kappa_move*theta
       <= center_cov+sqrt(alpha_res*beta_res).              (20)

To prove necessity of the column-space conditions, a vector in the
kernel of A_piv has zero quadratic energy in (18), so its mixed terms
must vanish. For sufficiency and the remaining necessity, complete the
square in the pivot variables. In the nonsingular case this is ordinary
matrix completion of the square. In the rank1 cases only the displayed
positive scalar pivot is used; the other pivot direction has zero mixed
terms by the column-space requirements. In rank0 there is no pivot term.
The remaining quadratic form on the last two coordinates is exactly

    [[alpha_res,z(theta)-center_cov],
     [z(theta)-center_cov,beta_res]].

A real symmetric2by2 matrix is PSD exactly when both diagonal entries
are nonnegative and the squared off-diagonal is at most their product.
This proves (19)--(20), including alpha_res=0 or beta_res=0. In either
zero-energy case the covariance is forced to center_cov; there is no
division by a vanishing energy and no strict-complementarity assumption.

Let theta_PSD_low and theta_PSD_high be the smaller and larger of

    (center_cov-sqrt(alpha_res*beta_res)-z_base)/kappa_move,
    (center_cov+sqrt(alpha_res*beta_res)-z_base)/kappa_move.   (21)

The ENTIRE ORIGINAL full-certificate fiber (12) is feasible if and
only if the sixteen unchanged signs, the pivot conditions, and (19)
hold and

    max(theta_sign_low,theta_PSD_low)
       <= min(theta_sign_high,theta_PSD_high).             (22)

When they hold, either endpoint or any point of this closed intersection
gives an exact array with all20 signs, original flow, and fullR5 PSD.
When they fail, no array ON THIS FIBER can be a full certificate. This
does not exclude other fibers or the whole cone.

If p2=p3, the basis argument above fails: one must NOT substitute
kappa_move=0 into (21). The following independent basis gives a complete
replacement, including zero steps, rather than deleting those regimes.

### 4.1. An exact fallback retaining zero steps and dependent quadratic columns

This subsection allows ANY w=h2>=0 and v=h3>=0. Replace theta by
w*tau when w>0, and define the motion directly for every w,v by

    Delta Lambda23=(w+v)*tau, Delta Lambda24=-w*tau,
    Delta Lambda32=v*tau,    Delta Lambda34=w*tau.           (23a)

Its Laplacian is tau*(e2-e3)*[v*e2-(w+v)*e3+w*e4]^T.
The right factor annihilates e and P, since
v*(P2-P3)+w*(P4-P3)=-v*w+w*v=0. Therefore exact flow and Me=0
are preserved even at w=0 or v=0. No division by either step occurs.
The exact twenty signs are the sixteen unchanged signs together with

    Lambda23^0+(w+v)*tau>=0, Lambda24^0-w*tau>=0,
    Lambda32^0+v*tau>=0,     Lambda34^0+w*tau>=0.           (23b)

The columns e,e0,e1,e2,e3 ALWAYS form a basis of R5. Thus with Me=0,
full PSD is equivalent to the leading4by4 block in e0,e1,e2,e3.
Its first2by2 pivot is the same A_piv as above. Its two mixed columns

    U2=(M02^0,M12^0)^T, U3=(M03^0,M13^0)^T

are fixed by the motion. Require A_piv>=0 and U2,U3 in its column
space. Use the same rank0/rank1/rank2 cost formulas as above, now
with r_vec=U2 and q_vec=U3. If the costs are c22,c33,c23, set

    R_two=[[r,s23],[s23,t]],
    r=M22^0-c22, t=M33^0-c33, s23=M23^0-c23.

Completing the square in the pivot reduces full PSD exactly to

    R_two+tau*[[j_move,k_move],[k_move,l_move]]>=0,
    j_move=v*(1-w), k_move=-j_move-w/2, l_move=j_move+w.    (23c)

To check the formula, the last three entries of the right factor
after multiplication by T^T are (j_move,-w-j_move,w).
Symmetrizing with e2-e3 gives precisely (23c). The determinant of
its coefficient matrix is -w^2/4, including the zero value at w=0.

Write

    d_zero=r*t-s23^2,
    d_one=r*l_move+t*j_move-2*s23*k_move.

The COMPLETE remaining PSD conditions are

    r+j_move*tau>=0, t+l_move*tau>=0,
    d_zero+d_one*tau-w^2*tau^2/4>=0.                      (23d)

For w>0, if d_one^2+w^2*d_zero<0 the fiber is infeasible.
Otherwise the last condition is exactly the closed interval with endpoints

    2*(d_one-sqrt(d_one^2+w^2*d_zero))/w^2,
    2*(d_one+sqrt(d_one^2+w^2*d_zero))/w^2.                (23e)

For w=0, the last condition in (23d) is the LINEAR inequality
d_zero+d_one*tau>=0. Intersect it with the two displayed diagonal
conditions and (23b). Every linear inequality A*tau+B>=0 is processed
by sign: for A>0 it is a lower bound, for A<0 an upper bound, and
for A=0 it requires B>=0 without division. This also covers w=v=0,
when the motion is zero and the conditions simply test the unchanged
full array. All singular-pivot range conditions remain in force.

Therefore (23b)--(23e), the sixteen unchanged signs, and the pivot/range
conditions decide the entire original fiber for every nonnegative h.
They preserve every original formal gradient at zero steps. On the
nondegenerate domain they are equivalent to (17)--(22), because both
are exact congruence tests of the same full matrix. This extension adds
no positivity, reversal, saturation, or formal-coordinate-identification
assumption to the original problem.

## 5. This is an actual full-cone freedom, not just a formal covariance

We give a symbolic example of two distinct full certificates on the same
word for which all the preserved data in Section3 agree and the terminal
covariance differs. This tests the false shortcut that those data already
determine the terminal covariance. The example has small capacity; it is
NOT a threshold seed or a claim to survive high-capacity screening.

Set epsilon=1/10000 and h_i=epsilon for i<4. Thus P_i=i*epsilon.
Let mu=7*epsilon/2 and define the five positive weights

    wgt=(4/7,4/5,4/3,4,16), Wgt=sum_i wgt_i=2384/105<24,
    phi_i=1+i*epsilon,
    Lambda^c_ij=phi_i*wgt_i*wgt_j/Wgt for i!=j.              (23)

For clarity these wgt_i are weights, not the step w=h2 in Section3.
They satisfy

    wgt_i=2h_i/(mu-P_i), i<4,
    wgt_4=2s/(s-mu),
    sum_i wgt_i(P_i-mu)=0.

Consequently sum_i wgt_i P_i=mu Wgt and
sum_i phi_i wgt_i=(1+mu)Wgt. The outgoing minus incoming mass at i
in (23) is wgt_i(P_i-mu)=omega_i, with the prescribed sign.
Its row moment is phi_i*wgt_i*(mu-P_i), which equals the relevant
right side of (3), including -2sC for the last row. Hence the base
array has the full flow and M^c e=0. All20 signs are strictly positive.

It remains to prove full PSD, rather than infer it from those facts.
Let

    L_zero=diag(wgt)-wgt*wgt^T/Wgt,
    J_index=diag(0,1,2,3,4),
    N_low be the5by5 matrix with ones strictly below the diagonal
          and zeros elsewhere.

Then L^c=(I+epsilon*J_index)L_zero and T=I+epsilon*N_low.
Let D_one=diag(2,2,2,2,-8) and D_two=diag(-1,-1,-1,-1,-16).
The exact matrix expansion is

    M^c=L_zero
       +epsilon*(D_one+sym(J_index*L_zero+L_zero*N_low))
       +epsilon^2*(D_two+sym(J_index*L_zero*N_low)).        (24)

For any z perpendicular to e,

    z^T L_zero z
      =sum_(i<j)wgt_i*wgt_j*(z_i-z_j)^2/Wgt
      >=(16/(49*24))*5*||z||^2
      =(10/147)||z||^2>(1/15)||z||^2.                   (25)

Here min_i wgt_i=4/7 and sum_(i<j)(z_i-z_j)^2=5||z||^2.
Also 0<=L_zero<=diag(wgt), so its Euclidean operator norm is at
most16. The norms of J_index and N_low are at most4; for N_low,
the sum of squared entries is10, so even its Frobenius norm is below4.
The norms of D_one,D_two are8,16. Thus (24) gives

    ||M^c-L_zero||<=136*epsilon+272*epsilon^2<1/50.         (26)

Equations (25)--(26) prove that M^c is positive definite on e-perp
with lower bound7/150, and Me=0 supplies PSD on allR5.

In (12), v=w=epsilon, so the four coefficient changes are
2theta,-theta,theta,theta. Every base coefficient is at least
16/(49*24)=2/147. For either theta=epsilon or theta=-epsilon,
each changed coefficient remains positive because 2epsilon<2/147.
The sixteen other coefficients stay positive.

In (14) the nonzero t_move coordinates are
(1-epsilon,-2+epsilon,1). Its norm is below sqrt(6), so
||J_move||<=||v_move||*||t_move||<sqrt(12)<4.
For either sign of theta=epsilon, the perturbation norm is below
4epsilon<1/1000. The lower bound7/150 from (26) remains positive.
Both arrays therefore satisfy all20 signs, exact flow, and fullR5 PSD.

For this word p_i=(1-epsilon)^i and
p2-p3=epsilon*(1-epsilon)^2>0. The terminal covariances of the two
arrays differ by

    epsilon*(p2-p3)=epsilon^2*(1-epsilon)^2>0.             (27)

Every quantity listed as invariant in Section3 agrees exactly. This is
a full-cone counterexample to covariance determination by those data.
The first-row positive-excess hypotheses of (5) do not hold for this
small-step example, and no such claim is made. The algebraic invariance
(12)--(16), and the full-fiber decision (22), DO apply in the intended
positive-excess regime. If a threshold fiber is sign-saturated or has
a singular PSD interval, it can have only one allowed theta or none;
the small-step example does not imply an open interval there.

## 6. Connection to the complete full-support coordinates

This section identifies exactly how the fiber sits in the ORIGINAL full
cone. It does not reduce the original problem to the one chosen center.
For simplicity assume all h_i>0, as in the positive-excess target regime.
Choose P3<mu<s and set

    phi_i=1+P_i,
    wgt_i=2h_i/(mu-P_i), i<4; wgt_4=2s/(s-mu),
    Wgt=sum_i wgt_i,
    Lambda^c_ij=phi_i*wgt_i*wgt_j/Wgt, i!=j.               (28)

The same two weighted-sum calculations used for (23) prove exact flow,
Me=0 and strict positivity of all20 coefficients. PSD is a separate
requirement. With zero steps, choose mu between the last prefix preceding
a positive step and s, and give zero weights to zero-step indices;
the algebra still works, but strict positivity is no longer asserted.

Define the5by4 matrix E_diff with columns e_i-e4, i0 through3,
and the5by3 matrix F_null with columns

    e_j-(1-P_j/s)e0-(P_j/s)e4, j1 through3.

The columns of E_diff are a basis of e-perp. Those of F_null are
independent from their middle coordinates and are a basis of the vectors
annihilating both e and P under transpose. Every real flow/kernel
completion, without imposing signs yet, is uniquely

    L=L^c+E_diff*X*F_null^T, X any real4by3 matrix.          (29)

Indeed the difference must satisfy Delta e=0, Delta^T e=0 and Delta P=0;
the last identity follows from (M-M^c)e=Delta P/2. Expanding columns
and rows in the two displayed bases proves (29) in both directions.
The original signs are exactly

    (E_diff*X*F_null^T)_ij<=Lambda^c_ij, every i!=j.         (30)

Let B_pick have columns e0,e1,e2,e3, set R=F_null^T*T*B_pick,
and K_c=B_pick^T*M^c*B_pick. Then

    K=K_c+sym(XR),
    M=[[K,-K*1],[-1^T*K,1^T*K*1]].                        (31)

Thus K>=0 is equivalent to full M>=0, not a substitute for (30).
Let q_diff=(p0-p4,p1-p4,p2-p4,p3-p4)^T. The identity
T^(-1)P=e-p gives ker(F_null^T T)=span(e,p), and restriction to
columns with terminal coordinate0 gives rank(R)=3 and
ker(R)=span(q_diff). Here q_diff!=0 when s>0, since otherwise
p=e and the recurrence would force all h_i=0. Calibration (4) becomes

    q_diff^T K q_diff=Qenergy.                            (32)

For completeness, if a proposed symmetric K has (32), put Hdiff=K-K_c
and

    S_zero=[q_diff*(Hdiff*q_diff)^T
              -(Hdiff*q_diff)*q_diff^T]/(q_diff^T*q_diff).

Then S_zero*q_diff=-Hdiff*q_diff. All real completions of this K are

    X=(Hdiff+S_zero+Z)*R^T*(RR^T)^(-1),
    Z^T=-Z, Z*q_diff=0.                                  (33)

The inverse is valid because rank(R)=3. Multiplication by R verifies
(33), as R^T(RR^T)^(-1)R projects onto q_diff-perp. Conversely XR-Hdiff
is skew, annihilates q_diff after adding Hdiff, and must have the
displayed form. The remaining Z-space is the three-dimensional skew
space on q_diff-perp. Every one of the20 signs (30) is still required.
If Qenergy=0 AND K>=0 then K*q_diff=0; otherwise no second kernel
is imposed. This reproduces the relevant complete-coordinate proof,
rather than treating draft443f4b4325a9901d as an accepted premise.

The motion (13) is a NONZERO member of the12-dimensional space (29).
It therefore may be chosen as one direction in a basis of that space.
For each fixed choice of the other11 real coordinates, Sections3--4
decide its entire remaining theta fiber by (17)--(22), or by the complete
fallback (23a)--(23e), provided the unchanged signs and pivot conditions
are checked. This is an exact
elimination of one full-interface parameter. It is not a restriction
to X=0, proportional coefficients, rank1 K, or a fixed skew triple.

It also explains the remaining difficulty precisely. The entries of
A_piv,r_vec,q_vec,n and the sixteen unchanged signs depend on those
other11 coordinates and on h. Their feasible high-capacity joint range
is not determined by (5)--(9). Choosing the positive center does not
prove its pivot or residual energies nonnegative. Choosing a calibrated
PSD K does not prove (30). Choosing a nonempty sign interval does not
prove the PSD interval meets it. Each of these missing matches can fail
independently; none is supplied by the unproved general reversal theorem.

## 7. Direct-proof screens, exact scope and handoff

Three routes were screened in this round.

1. Terminal-cut energy plus the unit quadratic alone: substituting a
   quadratic prefix and constant formal terminal gradient simply recovers
   the already calibrated identity (4). No additional obstruction is
   claimed from that computation.
2. Infer the terminal covariance from the initial lifted cut and endpoint
   budgets: false as an algebraic determination claim. The actual
   four-edge family (12), and the two fully feasible arrays in Section5,
   give a precise counterexample. The missing term is exactly the incoming
   weighted terminal sum in (10).
3. Keep that covariance and impose full PSD jointly: complete on every
   fiber (12) with p2!=p3, with the full alternative (23a)--(23e) also
   covering dependent quadratic columns and zero steps. The exact
   range/pivot conditions and interval intersections retain all20 signs
   and all of R5. Zero and saturated residual energies are handled
   explicitly, without division by them.

Here is the full redundancy calculation for the first screen. Put
Apre=1+P3=C-v, d_last=v-1, and
w_const=(p0,p1,p2,p3,p3)^T. Give the first four samples their actual
unit-quadratic prefix jets. Append a FORMAL constant-gradient step with
g4=g3=p3 and f4=f3-v*p3^2. In (2), V_h then equals the prefix energy
Qpre=1-Apre^2*p3^2. All prefix Q vanish, outgoing terminal residuals
vanish, and Q_i4=v*(p3-p_i)*p3 for i<3. Hence

    Qpre=w_const^T M w_const
             -v*p3*sum_(i<3)Lambda_i4*(p3-p_i).             (34)

The direct original off-diagonal identity is

    Lambda_i4=(h_i-1)*y_i+h_i*sum_(j<i)y_j-2*M_i4.

For each j<3, telescoping h_i*p_i=p_i-p_(i+1) gives

    sum_(i=j)^2 h_i*(p3-p_i)-(p3-p_j)=(P3-P_j)*p3.

Also Me=0 gives sum_(i<3)M_i4*(p3-p_i)=-w_const^T M e4.
Thus the sum in (34) is

    p3*sum_(j<3)(P3-P_j)*y_j+2*w_const^T M e4.

Complete the square in (34) and use (9). Because
w_const-v*p3*e4=p, the result is exactly

    Qpre=Qenergy-s*v*delta_v*p3^2.                         (35)

This is already the polynomial identity
Apre^2-C^2*d_last^2=s*v*delta_v, since
Apre-C*d_last=delta_v and Apre+C*d_last=s*v. The formal extension
in this calculation is not asserted to be an actual function. It is
used only inside the exact full formal identity (2). Accordingly this
specific attempted extra test is redundant, rather than a new witness.

This resolves the covariance/sign/PSD interface along an original-array
direction and identifies the uncovered full-interface freedom. It does
not decide the remaining high-capacity step region, construct an actual
C>=193/25 seed, prove cone reversal, or prove full-cone nonexistence.
No excluded endpoint square/prism is re-scanned or used as a premise.
Capacity C is a variable throughout Sections1--4 and6; only the separate
counterexample of Section5 fixes a small-step word.

All derivations are textual and exact. No numerical/model evaluation,
solver, finite replay, machine algebra, installation, consult, subordinate
agent or fact_submit was used. The initial Matlas retrieval returned
unrelated noisy-GD/constrained-dynamics results; web fallback recovered
the already audited composition literature. A later targeted matrix
query found the generalized Schur-complement statement in Zhaosong Lu
and Ting Kei Pong, "Computing Optimal Experimental Designs via Interior
Point Method", arXiv:1009.1909v3 (2012), Lemma3.1. Their notation uses
the Moore-Penrose inverse C^dagger and the column-space requirement:
for symmetric A,C, the block [[A,B^T],[B,C]] is PSD exactly when
C>=0, Range(B) is contained in Range(C), and A-B^T C^dagger B>=0.
This is background matching, not an imported premise: Section4 proves
the required rank0/rank1/rank2 version directly. The exact PDF was
downloaded; the local pdftotext executable was absent. The arXiv HTML
was then saved and extracted with the standard-library HTML parser,
and Section1.1 plus Lemma3.1 and its surrounding application were read.
The paper states this lemma with a book reference, without a proof of
the lemma itself; no claim to have read a missing proof is made.

Appendix A reproduces the earlier first-row proof so that the new joint
draft preserves its full hypotheses and quantitative bound. The source
draft's discovery provenance is not copied or used as a mathematical
premise. Formal verification remains a separately selected future gate.


## Appendix A. Reproduced joint first-row proof

This is the proof text of Sections1--7 of the earlier local draft.
All assertions remain part of this unverified joint candidate.

### 1. Statement and notation

Write a positive-excess four-step word as

    h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,1+d),    a,b,c,d>0,
    C=1+sum h=5+a+b+c+d.

Suppose it admits the full primitive certificate of accepted fact
04219554e41d9750. Suppose additionally that a>sqrt(2)-1. Define

    u=1+a,             delta=u^2-2,
    H=3+c+d,           eta=b/H,
    R=a+3+1/a,         k=u*delta/a,
    nu=(1+eta)*(1-a*eta)/a,
    Erow=R-k*eta-C^2*eta^2,
    Qenergy=1-C^2*a^2*b^2*c^2*d^2,
    Wrow=max(1+b,1+b*c*d),
    Krow=max(u,a*Wrow).

Full feasibility forces a<1, a*eta<1, nu>0, and
0<=Qenergy<1. The following quantitative necessary condition holds:

    u*delta <= Krow*Erow/nu + 2*sqrt(Qenergy*Erow/nu).          (A)

In particular,

    Erow >= nu * [u*delta /
          (sqrt(Qenergy+Krow*u*delta)+sqrt(Qenergy))]^2 > 0.  (B)

Thus equality in the previously used lifted middle-cut condition Erow>=0
is impossible throughout this initial-step regime. A version avoiding
square roots is

    Erow>=0,
    [max(u*delta*nu-Krow*Erow,0)]^2 <= 4*Qenergy*Erow*nu.     (C)

If a*b<=1 and c*d<=1, the simpler strict necessary inequality is

    Erow > nu*delta^2/u^2,                                 (D)

equivalently

    b < (a+3)*(C-a-2) / ((a+1)*(C+a+2)).                (E)

The last two product assumptions are explicit hypotheses of this
simplification. They are not silently declared lossless for the
original problem. Conditions(A)--(C) do not require them. The separate
assumption a,b,c,d>0 is also retained explicitly; no assertion about a
zero step or a step at most one is being imported from an unverified
compactness draft.

### 2. Original formal certificate and its constant kernel

Put P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, s=P_4=C-1, and let e be the five-entry all-ones
column. Write e_i for the i-th original standard coordinate of R^5,
with indices0 through4. Let T have diagonal1, entry h_t at(i,t) for t<i, and zero
entries above the diagonal. The original twenty multipliers satisfy

    Lambda_ij>=0 for i!=j, Lambda_ii=0,
    L=diag(Lambda*e)-Lambda,
    omega=(-2h0,-2h1,-2h2,-2h3,2s)^T,
    D=diag(h0(2-h0),h1(2-h1),h2(2-h2),h3(2-h3),-s(s+2)),
    L^T e=omega,
    M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2 >=0 on ALL of R^5.               (1)

The identity in accepted04219554e41d9750 is

    V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
                      =sum_(i,j) M_ij <g_i,g_j>,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    V_h=2sum_(i<4)h_i(f_i-f4)
          +sum_(i<4)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g4||^2,
    x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i.                                (2)

Since Te=e+P and sum_(i<4)h_i P_i=(s^2-sum h_i^2)/2,

    e^TDe=-s^2-sum h_i^2,
    omega^TTe=s^2+sum h_i^2,
    e^TMe=0.

For every real v, the nonnegative quadratic(e+t*v)^T M(e+t*v)
has zero constant coefficient. Considering both signs of t forces
v^TMe=0, hence Me=0. The initial row of this identity is

    sum_(j>0)Lambda_0j P_j=2h0.                        (3)

To check(3) without any suppressed flow condition, direct expansion
gives(Me)_i=h_i(1+P_i)+(LP)_i/2 for i<4. At i=0,
P0=0 and(LP)_0=-sum_(j>0)Lambda_0j P_j.

Let Aout=sum_(j>0)Lambda_0j and m=M00. Its original diagonal is

    m=1-a^2-a*Aout.                                    (4)

Indeed the first column of T is(1,u,u,u,u)^T, so(LT)_00
=(1-u)Aout=-a*Aout. Since m,Aout>=0 and a>0, (4) gives
a<=1. Equality would give m=Aout=0, contradicting(3). Thus a<1.

For any positive semidefinite real matrix, the two-vector inequality

    |v^T M w|^2 <= (v^T M v)*(w^T M w)                 (5)

follows by applying nonnegativity to(v+t*w)^T M(v+t*w).
If w^T M w=0, the linear coefficient must vanish; otherwise minimizing
in t proves(5). This argument does not assume positive definiteness.

### 3. Two exact scalar energies

Here clamp(x,[-eta,1]) means min(1,max(-eta,x)).
The scalar function with this derivative and minimum0
has the actual trajectory

    x=(2+a,1,-b,-(2+d)*eta,-eta),
    g=(1,1,-eta,-eta,-eta).

Here b=(3+c+d)*eta. Its derivative is continuous, nondecreasing, and
1-Lipschitz. At every displayed sample its value is
f_i=x_i*g_i-g_i^2/2. The first step gives x1=1; the other three GD
updates follow directly from the displayed relation for b.

For any GD data with saturated values
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2, telescoping the squared distances gives

    V_h=||x0||^2-||x4+s*g4||^2.                        (6)

In detail, the first two sums in(2) reduce to
2sum h_i<x_i,g_i>-sum h_i^2||g_i||^2-2s*f4;
the first two terms telescope to||x0||^2-||x4||^2.
Substitution of f4 then gives(6). The scalar trajectory has x4=g4,
so its energy is(2+a)^2-C^2*eta^2. By(1)--(2) it is nonnegative.
Consequently

    C*eta<=2+a,
    a*eta<=a*(2+a)/C<3/5<1,                           (7)

because 0<a<1 and C>5. This proves nu>0.

Next define the actual unit-quadratic gradient column

    p=(1,-a,a*b,-a*b*c,a*b*c*d)^T.

It satisfies Tp=e. Since Le=0,

    p^T M p=p^T D p=1-C^2*p4^2=Qenergy.               (8)

For the telescoping equality, p_(i+1)=(1-h_i)p_i gives
h_i(2-h_i)p_i^2=p_i^2-p_(i+1)^2; the terminal diagonal
then supplies-s(s+2)p4^2. Full PSD gives Qenergy>=0.
All a,b,c,d are positive, so Qenergy<1.

### 4. Complete lifted-cut data and its exact multiplier cost

Let U=(1,0), V=(0,1) be orthonormal physical vectors. Set

    z=-eta*U+sqrt(nu)*V.

Then

    ||z||^2=[1+(1-a)*eta]/a,
    <U,z>+a||z||^2=1-a*eta.

Use the five two-dimensional data

    X=(U+u*z, U, -b*U, -(2+d)*eta*U, -eta*U),
    G=(z, U, -eta*U, -eta*U, -eta*U),
    F_i=<X_i,G_i>-||G_i||^2/2.                         (9)

Every GD update has the original step length. For these saturated
values the original ordered interpolation residual is exactly

    Q_ij=<G_i-G_j,X_j-G_j>.

Here is a check of all twenty ordered finite pairs, grouped by their
target j. Targets1 and4 have zero normal. At target0 the normal is
U+a*z. The incoming pair(1,0) has residual0, whereas each incoming
pair(i,0), i=2,3,4, has residual-a*nu. At target2 the normal is
-(b-eta)U. The only nonzero finite residual there is
Q_12=-(1+eta)(b-eta). At target3 the normal is
-(1+d)*eta*U. The only nonzero finite residual there is
Q_13=-(1+eta)(1+d)*eta. Thus all twenty residuals are nonpositive.
The zero optimum jet can also be added: Q_(i,star)=0 by the chosen
values; the opposite inequalities follow from the same normals and
a*eta<1. These optimum pairs are not additional multipliers in(1).

For completeness these are actual jets of one global smooth convex
function. Let Kphys=conv{0,z,U,-eta*U} and define

    F(X)=max_(G in Kphys)(<X,G>-||G||^2/2).

The comparisons just made say that X_i-G_i is an outward normal
to Kphys at G_i. Completing the square identifies the unique maximizer
as the Euclidean projection of X onto Kphys. If projections at X,Y
are G,H, adding their normal inequalities gives
||G-H||^2<=<G-H,X-Y>, and hence ||G-H||<=||X-Y||.
To see differentiability directly, optimality at X and X+v bounds
F(X+v)-F(X)-<G,v> between0 and<G(X+v)-G,v>, which is at most||v||^2.
Thus the gradient is the projection and is1-Lipschitz. The function
is convex as a maximum of affine functions of X; it has minimum0 at0
because0 belongs to Kphys. It realizes all of(9).

By(6), its primitive energy is

    ||U+u*z||^2-C^2*eta^2
      =a+3+1/a-(u*delta/a)*eta-C^2*eta^2=Erow.            (10)

Let

    Bin=Lambda20+Lambda30+Lambda40.

In the U coordinate the gradient column is-eta*e+(1+eta)*e1;
in the V coordinate it is sqrt(nu)*e0. The constant kernel proved
in Section2 therefore gives the full matrix remainder
(1+eta)^2*M11+nu*m. Substituting every nonzero residual above into
the original identity(2) yields the exact accounting identity

    Erow=nu*(m+a*Bin)+(1+eta)^2*M11
       +(1+eta)*eta*((2+c+d)*Lambda12+(1+d)*Lambda13). (11)

Every term on the right is nonnegative. In particular

    0<=m<=m+a*Bin<=Erow/nu.                              (12)

No incoming coefficient has been set to zero. Identity(11) prices the
original incoming coefficients explicitly.

### 5. The original first row couples the two energies

Define the weighted incoming combination

    Bweight=(1+b)*Lambda20+(1-b*c)*Lambda30
                                    +(1+b*c*d)*Lambda40.

The exact first-row identity is

    2*e0^T M p = u*(m-delta)+a^2*Bweight.             (13)

Here e0 denotes the original first coordinate column of R5. A direct
derivation avoids any coordinate reduction. Since Tp=e and Le=0,
Mp=Dp+T^T L^T p/2. Also Te0=u*e-a*e0, giving

    2*e0^T M p=2(1-a^2)-a*p^T L e0.

The flow at node0 gives Lambda10=Aout+2u-Bin. Hence

    p^T L e0
      =Aout+a*Lambda10-a*b*Lambda20
                        +a*b*c*Lambda30-a*b*c*d*Lambda40
      =u*Aout+2a*u-a*Bweight.

Finally substitute a*Aout=1-a^2-m from(4). The constant term becomes

    (2-u)(1-a^2)-2a^2*u
      =u*((1-a)^2-2a^2)=-u*delta,

which proves(13), with all row and flow contributions retained.

All three incoming multipliers are nonnegative. Since b,c,d>0,
each coefficient in Bweight is at most Wrow. This remains true even
if 1-b*c is negative. Thus Bweight<=Wrow*Bin. Applying(5) with e0,p,
and using(8), gives from(13)

    u*delta <= u*m+a^2*Wrow*Bin+2*sqrt(m*Qenergy)
             <= Krow*(m+a*Bin)+2*sqrt(m*Qenergy)
             <= Krow*Erow/nu+2*sqrt(Qenergy*Erow/nu).        (14)

This is(A). Put t=sqrt(Erow/nu). Because Krow,u,delta are positive,
(14) says

    Krow*t^2+2*sqrt(Qenergy)*t-u*delta>=0.

Solving this increasing quadratic for t>=0 and rationalizing its
positive root gives(B). Its numerator is strictly positive, so Erow>0.
Multiplying(A) by nu and squaring only when its left side after
subtraction is positive gives exactly(C). There is no sign-losing
squaring in this formulation.

### 6. A rational strengthened middle-cut boundary

If c*d<=1, then Wrow=1+b. If also a*b<=1, then
a*Wrow=a+a*b<=1+a=u, so Krow=u. Since Qenergy<1 and Erow>0,
(14) implies the strict inequality

    u*delta < u*t^2+2t,       t=sqrt(Erow/nu)>0.

The right side is strictly increasing for t>=0. Its value at
t=delta/u is

    delta^2/u+2delta/u=delta*(delta+2)/u=u*delta,

because delta+2=u^2. It follows that t>delta/u, which is(D).
The exact algebraic identity

    Erow-nu*delta^2/u^2
      =[(a+3-delta*eta)^2-u^2*C^2*eta^2]/u^2          (15)

can be checked by expanding the constant, linear, and quadratic
coefficients in eta. Those coefficients in the numerator are
(a+3)^2, -2(a+3)*delta, and delta^2-u^2*C^2.

The first factor a+3-delta*eta is positive: by a*eta<1,

    a+3-delta*eta > a+3-delta/a = 1+1/a >0.

Thus(D) and(15) give

    a+3 > (u*C+delta)*eta.

Substitute eta=b/(C-2-a-b), whose denominator is H>0. Since
delta+a+3=(a+1)(a+2), the result is exactly(E). Every denominator
in(E) is positive.

In particular this is a necessary bound on an entire step region,
not a condition on a sparse coefficient family. The exact stronger
condition(A) still retains the actual Qenergy rather than discarding
it in favor of the upper bound1.

### 7. Adversarial scope checks

The initial-step hypothesis is substantive. At a=sqrt(2)-1 the
quantity delta is zero, and(A) supplies no positive gap. This agrees
with the possibility of saturated basic certificates at that endpoint.
We do not extend the strict conclusion by continuity across delta=0.

If b*c>1, the coefficient of Lambda30 in(13) is negative. The proof
does not replace this coefficient by its absolute value or infer a
false lower bound. It uses only the valid upper bound Bweight<=Wrow*Bin.
Thus(A)--(C) still apply in that regime. The simplified statement(E)
requires only its explicitly stated a*b and c*d hypotheses.

If Qenergy=0, formula(B) reduces to Erow>=nu*u*delta/Krow;
no division by Qenergy occurs. If a multiplier vanishes or M has
additional kernels, the semidefinite form of(5) still applies. No
strict complementarity, full rank, or strictly positive support was used.

Here is a purely symbolic test family showing why the separate tests
Erow>=0 and Qenergy>=0 cannot justify the omitted coupling. It is an
analytical counterexample to that shortcut, not a numerical seed proposal
or a claim to survive the other existing necessary inequalities. Take

    a=1/2, eta=7/24, C=39*sqrt(2)/7,
    H=24*(C-5/2)/31, b=7*(C-5/2)/31, Ktail=H-3,
    0<d<min(Ktail/2,1/Ktail,1/(C*a*b*Ktail)), c=Ktail-d.

The elementary bounds 7/5<sqrt(2)<10/7 give
193/25<39/5<C<8. Thus Ktail>0, all four excesses are positive,
a*b<1, c*d<1, and 0<C*a*b*c*d<1. Also C=5+a+b+c+d
and eta=b/(3+c+d). Direct exact substitution gives

    R-k*eta=169/32=C^2*eta^2,
    Erow=0, Qenergy>0,
    nu=1271/576, delta=1/4, u=3/2.

Yet(D) requires Erow>1271/20736. Hence none of these words has the
full certificate. This illustrates the active original-row obstruction;
it is not asserted to be a newly uncovered region beyond every prior
obstruction. No finite numerical values were evaluated for this family.

# Max46: a persistent reset-state kernel and its original-word obstruction

Status: complete analytical draft, UNVERIFIED; no fact_id and no formal
submission. This is the first of the two newly allocated research rounds
after completed45. The original all-word/all-large-horizon goal remains
open. The assignment expressly forbids fact_submit in this launch.

## 1. Exact question, evidence, and the changed hypothesis

For each enclosing integer m fix, throughout every child,

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/m, beta=1-epsilon,
    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), j<m,
    h=H_m, n=2^m-1.

The target is still the original unrestricted W=2R exact-profile question,
not a result limited to these words. The present alternative deliverable
is an exact obstruction to a genuinely different, evidence-informed
template on the actual H_m words.

Accepted fact e9a55f57461f253c supplies a separate, exact FINITE actual-H6
witness. It has signed gradient correlations and cannot be represented
with every coordinate changing its gradient at most six times. Its
dimension66 is not its initial radius. None of those assertions is
extrapolated to a child with a different enclosing m.

The frozen numerical evidence was read through the authorized broker:

* Manifest SHA256:
  0325f4761017bb8ea28086edd6491962594eb9817284a5b03b86b2c4e203e7e9.
* H3/H4/H6 recorded solver status: optimal_inaccurate; H5: optimal.
* Job1 failed before input reading: ModuleNotFoundError: No module named
  'numpy'. It was charged0.06777734299976146 child-wall seconds.
* Job2, the standard-library rewrite, completed in0.11816428099700715s.
  Frozen script SHA7cca0ac3fd5d1da78f2afd0c7b721e6680cd1d2fac9ee96c0b74d6f2ba501824.
  Output: research/frozen_structure_20261001/
  job_02_max46_heights_correlations/raw_output.txt.
* Job3 completed in0.11759519200131763s. Frozen script
  SHA531bf1eb87f4b10854d4f599f15647f32cee357d2e3447b45c7ea388aec939f3.
  Output: research/frozen_structure_20261001/
  job_03_max46_proximal_memory/raw_output.txt.
* Total charged so far:3/4 jobs,0.30353681599808624s. No solver,
  new trajectory, benchmark recurrence, all-Q/PSD replay, machine-algebra
  job, or formal verification was run.

Job2 used ORIGINAL Gram/value arrays, not their mass-scaled variants.
For the zero optimum, the conjugate height

    phi_i=<x_i,g_i>-f_i-||g_i||^2/2=-Q_(i,star)

was read from stored residuals. It is distinct from the primal height
delta_i=f_i-||g_i||^2/2. In saved H6 the former is around10^-9 to10^-8,
whereas delta_32 is about0.30018. Job3 evaluated proximal-vector norms
as linear forms of the saved formal-vector Gram. At the saved H6 indices
7,15,23,31,39,47 these norms squared are small, while at55 and63 they are
about0.88505 and0.32327. No exact zero follows from these numbers.
Endpoint gradient norms both increase and decrease. Chronological
two-future conditional residuals still have remote covariance; e.g. the
residual at3 has normalized covariance about0.44025 with16. Such
regressions can be ill-conditioned and are not rank or independence proofs.

These observations motivate, but do not establish, the exact hypotheses:
conjugate saturation, selected returns to x=g, nonmonotone reset-state
norms, and persistent correlations across distant cells. They do not
motivate a terminal reset. The candidate below permits an arbitrary
terminal tail after its tested prefix.

The new dependence law is the explicit scale-indexed kernel

    <v_i,v_j>=min(t_i,t_j),       ||v_i||^2=t_i.

It is nested by norm, not chronological order. Norms may repeatedly rise
and fall, so earlier coordinates can be reactivated and used arbitrarily
often. There is no fixed bound on physical dimension or coordinate jump
count. Signed intermediate gradients will be negative combinations of
two reset states. This is not a one-future-gradient law, a capped signed
H2 innovation law, or the old power profile for nested dual normals.

One restriction is deliberately retained: each H2 half-cell has constant
gradient. The frozen H5/H6 data have appreciable changes INSIDE some H2
cells. Those changes are not reproduced by this first template; they are
one explicit remaining interface. Another is the exact min-kernel itself:
the frozen data do not prove it, and it is not a lossless Gram model.

## 2. Finite Euclidean realization of the new dependence law

For any finite nonnegative list t_i, sort its distinct positive values as
0<tau_1<...<tau_d and put tau_0=0. With orthonormal vectors e_1,...,e_d,
define

    v_i=sum_(ell:tau_ell<=t_i) sqrt(tau_ell-tau_(ell-1))*e_ell.

Then the common coordinates of v_i and v_j are precisely those with
tau_ell<=min(t_i,t_j). Their inner product telescopes to min(t_i,t_j),
and ||v_i||^2=t_i. Thus this is an explicit finite-dimensional generating
rule, not a PSD oracle. Equal t_i give identical vectors; increasing and
decreasing t_i turn coordinates on and off according to scale. Nothing
here establishes smooth interpolation of a prescribed GD transcript.

## 3. Original H3 cells, values, and intended static function

Set

    s=sum H_2=3+2a+beta,
    A=s+1=4+2a+beta, b=beta*c, D=A+b,
    E=A^2-2Ab-b^2, K=A^2(2A+b),
    B_lo=beta*c^2, B_hi=beta*c^3.

Here B_lo and B_hi are bridge EXCESSES, not smoothness constants or recursive
words. Smoothness is normalized to1. In an H3 cell, the first H2 is
followed by the physical step1+b and then the second H2. Its seven
steps sum to2s+1+b.

At cell j, physical indices8j,...,8j+7, prescribe u_j as gradient at
8j,...,8j+3 and v_j at8j+4,...,8j+7. Prescribe the reset x_(8j+7)=v_j.
The second H2 then starts at A*v_j. At its preceding central query,

    x_(8j+3)=(1+b)u_j+A*v_j,
    y_(8j+3):=x_(8j+3)-u_j=b*u_j+A*v_j,
    x_(8j)=D*u_j+A*v_j.

For j>=1, the original physical bridge8j-1 has excess

    B_j=beta*c^(2+nu_2(j)),

where nu_2(j) is the exponent of2 dividing j. Since the preceding reset
is v_(j-1), the actual GD update gives x_(8j)=-B_j*v_(j-1). Consequently

    u_j=-(B_j*v_(j-1)+A*v_j)/D.                 (1)

All positions inside each H2 are retained, via their actual partial sums;
no query is deleted. At every prescribed sample impose the conjugate
saturation value

    f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2.                  (2)

In particular a reset has value||v_j||^2/2. Include the optimum sample
x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For the ORIGINAL ordered residual convention of
accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290, direct expansion of(2) gives

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2
        =<g_i-g_j,x_j-g_j>.                    (3)

If every normal inequality(3) held, a natural intended single function
would be F(x)=max_(g in C)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2), where C is the convex hull
of0 and all supplied gradients. The inequalities say that g_i is the
Euclidean projection of x_i onto C, and hence give the values(2).
This intended construction is NOT asserted legal: the next sections
prove that an original row fails. No minimizer or radius is inferred from
the intended hull before interpolation is checked.

## 4. Local transition calculation and first explicit law

Consider one cell after a bridge of excess B>0. Write p=v_(j-1),v=v_j,
P=||p||^2>0,T=||v||^2, and use <p,v>=min(P,T). At the central query,

    u=-(B*p+A*v)/D,
    y=(-bB*p+A^2*v)/D.

The retained row with source gradient v and target gradient u is

    D^2*Q_(v,u)=K*T+E*B*min(P,T)-bB^2*P.       (4)

To derive(4), multiply
(v-u)=[B*p+(2A+b)*v]/D by y. The mixed coefficient is
B[A^2-b(2A+b)]=B*E, and the other two coefficients are-bB^2 and K.

For a shrinking endpoint T<=P, this row requires

    T/P<=alpha(B):=bB^2/(K+EB),                (5)

whenever the denominator is positive. For a growing endpoint T>=P it
requires

    T/P<=gamma(B):=B(bB-E)/K.                  (6)

The factorization

    K*(gamma(B)-1)=(bB-A^2)(B+2A+b)           (7)

shows where the two regimes meet. At B=A^2/b both bounds equal1.
The first explicit extrapolation law selected in plan6e9c5cb11fc77d32
was to take equality in(5) below this threshold and equality in(6)
above it, using the exact B_j and fixed enclosing-m parameters in every
cell. This produces nonmonotone reset norms and persistent state.

The following obstruction is STRONGER: it allows arbitrary reset norms
subject only to the local inequalities, not this maximal-transition law.
Thus lowering a selected factor or independently retuning all endpoint
norms cannot repair the entire declared min-kernel family.

## 5. Uniform parameter bounds on the actual words

For every integer m>=100, put epsilon=1/m. The elementary identities
lambda^2=2lambda+1 and lambda^3=2lambda^2+lambda will be used, together
with12/5<lambda<5/2. Put

    d=lambda^2-A=epsilon+4epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)].

Then0<d<(8/3)epsilon<=2/75. Direct substitution gives

    (99/100)lambda^2 < A < lambda^2,
    (99/100)lambda < b=lambda(1-epsilon^2) < lambda,
    lambda^2 < B_lo=lambda^2(1+epsilon-epsilon^2-epsilon^3)
                 <(101/100)lambda^2,
    lambda^3 < B_hi=lambda^3(1+2epsilon-2epsilon^3-epsilon^4)
                 <(102/100)lambda^3.           (8)

Non-strict versions of these bounds also suffice below. To see E<0,
factor E=(A-lambda*b)(A+b/lambda). Since d>=epsilon and
lambda^2*epsilon^2<epsilon, one has A<lambda*b. Moreover

    0<-E=(lambda*b-A)(A+b/lambda)
         <d(lambda^2+1)
         <(8/3)(1/100)(25/4+1)=29/150<1/5.    (9)

From(8) and2lambda^2+lambda=lambda^3,

    (97/100)lambda^7<K<lambda^7.              (10)

Indeed(99/100)^3>97/100. For the low bridge,
A^2>(99/100)^2 lambda^4 while b*B_lo<(101/100)lambda^3; since
(99/100)^2 lambda>101/100, we have b*B_lo<A^2. For the high bridge,

    b*B_hi=lambda^4[(1+epsilon)(1-epsilon^2)]^2
        >lambda^4>A^2,                        (11)

because epsilon-epsilon^2-epsilon^3>0.

Consequently define

    alpha=alpha(B_lo), gamma=gamma(B_hi).

Their denominators are positive. In detail,

    K+E*B_lo>(97/100)lambda^7-(101/500)lambda^2
           >(96/100)lambda^7,

where the last comparison uses lambda^5>101/5. Thus

    0<alpha<(11/10)lambda^(-2).               (12)

For gamma, the numerator B_hi(b*B_hi-E) is at most
(1.0404)lambda^7+(0.204)lambda^3<(1.05)lambda^7, since
lambda^4>85/4. Dividing by(10) gives gamma<11/10. Equation(11)
and(7) give gamma>1. Therefore

    1<gamma<11/10, alpha*gamma<121/(100lambda^2)<1.  (13)

The decimal constants in this paragraph are terminating rational numbers,
not numerical evaluations:1.0404=2601/2500,0.204=51/250,
1.05=21/20. No machine arithmetic is used in these inequalities.

## 6. Every nonzero profile fails a retained remote row

The obstruction uses only the original cells j=2 and j=3, preceded by
the reset at15. Their entering bridge excesses are

    h_15-1=B_hi, h_23-1=B_lo.

Their central bridge excess is b, at physical indices19 and27. Put

    t1=||v_1||^2, t2=||v_2||^2, t3=||v_3||^2.

Assume t1,t2>0 and that the local original rows Q_(20,19)<=0 and
Q_(28,27)<=0 hold. In the high cell, if t2<t1 its ratio is below1;
if t2>=t1, equation(6) applies. In either case

    t2/t1<=gamma<11/10.                       (14)

In the low cell, growth would contradict gamma(B_lo)<1, by(7) and b*B_lo<A^2.
Thus t3<t2, and(5) gives

    0<=t3/t2<=alpha<(11/10)lambda^(-2).        (15)

Equations(13)--(15) imply t3<t1. Set mu=min(t1,t2)>0. Then

    1<=t2/mu<11/10,
    <v_1,v_2>=mu,
    <v_1,v_3>=<v_2,v_3>=t3.                  (16)

The source gradient at16 and target gradient at27 are

    g_16=u_2=-(B_hi*v_1+A*v_2)/D,
    g_27=u_3=-(B_lo*v_2+A*v_3)/D,
    y_27=(-b*B_lo*v_2+A^2*v_3)/D.

Using(3) and(16), the ACTUAL ordered interpolation row is

    D^2*Q_(16,27)
      =b*B_lo*B_hi*mu+b*B_lo(A-B_lo)*t2
         +[A^2(B_lo-B_hi)-A*b*B_lo]*t3.              (17)

This row is retained in the original full objective problem. Source16
and target27 are both queried points, not an unqueried terminal pair.

We now bound(17) without choosing any norm profile. From(8),
b*B_lo*B_hi>(99/100)lambda^6. Also0<B_lo-A<(2/100)lambda^2. By(14)--(16),

    |b*B_lo(A-B_lo)*t2|/mu
       <(1.01)(0.02)(1.1)lambda^5
       <(1/100)lambda^6.                     (18)

The last coefficient in(17) is negative. Its magnitude, divided by mu,
is less than

    [A^2*B_hi+A*b*B_lo]*(t3/t2)*(t2/mu)
       <(1.21)[(1.02)lambda^5+(1.01)lambda^3]
       <(61/100)lambda^6.                    (19)

For the last strict inequality, divide by lambda^6 and use lambda>12/5:

    (1.21)[1.02/(12/5)+1.01/(12/5)^3]
       = (12342/10000)(5/12)
          +(12221/10000)(125/1728)
       <61/100.

This is a displayed rational comparison. Combining(17)--(19) gives

    D^2*Q_(16,27)>(37/100)lambda^6*mu
                           >(lambda^6/3)*mu.

Finally D<lambda^2+lambda and(lambda^2+lambda)^2=2lambda^4. Hence

    Q_(16,27)>(lambda^2/6)*mu>(9/10)*mu>0.    (20)

This contradicts the necessary interpolation inequality from accepted
fact1252b8823bb5a290. The conclusion is joint: either an earlier local
row fails, or this explicit remote row fails. PSD of the min-kernel Gram,
all GD updates, and passing the displayed local rows cannot substitute
for the missing global interpolation condition.

The proof allows EVERY positive scale t1,t2 and every t3 admitted by the
local rows. It does not assume equality in(5)--(6), a constant scale ratio,
fixed rank, a chronological ordering of the t_i, or any rule outside
these cells. Embedding the same vectors into a larger Euclidean space
preserves the offending row. Adding extra gradients to the intended hull
cannot remove an existing pair inequality.

## 7. Zero states, normalization, and exact scope of the obstruction

If t1=0, the prescribed reset has x_15=g_15=0. In any hypothetical smooth
convex interpolant with the zero optimum sample, every later GD iterate
would remain at that minimum. If t2=0, the same conclusion starts at23.
If t1,t2>0 and t3=0, the argument above still applies, with the last
term in(17) zero, so there is no exceptional repair at t3=0.

Therefore, for every actual H_m with m>=100, any LEGAL member retaining
these two constant-half H3 cells, the prescribed resets, saturated values,
and min-kernel reset correlations has zero terminal objective gap. There
is no positive-gap member, whatever earlier history or later tail is
chosen. About any true nearest minimizer with a nonzero initial distance,
its normalized payoff is0. This statement does not assert existence of
a nontrivial member or a particular initial radius.

For comparison on the same word, the complete accepted K/ray references
include the positive Huber value1/(1+2sum h), by facts b98bb807d514e80d
and1fca3e5dd555cb4a. Thus every legal member of the declared template has
payoff0, strictly below both references, for this unbounded actual-word
family. This also lies strictly below the UNaugmented FULL complex
reference C_full(h)=sup_(z in D_h) Gap_h(z) of accepted fact
dfde2fa326c908bf. Indeed put S=sum h and theta=1/[2(1+S)]. The real
quadratic F(x)=theta*x^2/2 has the exact real-parameter polynomial
transcript, so theta belongs to D_h and maximality of Gap_h gives
C_full(h)>=theta*product_(i<n)(1-theta*h_i)^2. Each theta*h_i is
nonnegative and their sum is below1/2. Induction using
(1-u)(1-v)>=1-u-v shows that the product is greater than1/2. Hence
C_full(h)>1/[8(1+S)]>0. This lower comparison uses the complete channel,
not a selected universal lens or an added Huber term.

The original all-split benchmark B_n is positive by its recurrence,
so its product with such a payoff is exactly0. This is a template
obstruction, NOT an upper bound on unrestricted W_n(H_m), and not an
asymptotic conclusion for the accepted finite H6 witness.

The hypotheses concerning values matter. The positive row(20) by itself
is NOT a value-independent cycle obstruction. Section8 derives a precise
necessary height cost when these values are relaxed. Nor does this result
close arbitrary kernels with the same three endpoint norms, nonradial returns, or changing
gradients inside the two H2 halves. Orthogonal addition of a separate
transcript can supply negative residuals and is not excluded by the
embedding statement above. Its value/radius cost would require a new
joint proof; no bound on all such sums is claimed.

## 8. Precise remaining interface and handoff

First retain the same min-kernel geometry and prescribed resets, but allow
arbitrary values belonging to one hypothetical legal smooth interpolant.
Define phi_i=<x_i,g_i>-f_i-||g_i||^2/2. The optimum row Q_(i,star)<=0
gives phi_i>=0. At a reset x_i=g_i, the two optimum rows give both
f_i<=||g_i||^2/2 and f_i>=||g_i||^2/2, so phi_i=0.

If two queried gradients are equal, their two interpolation inequalities
give f_i-f_j=<g_i,x_i-x_j>. Therefore phi is constant along each constant
H2 half. In particular phi_20=phi_23=0 and phi_28=phi_31=0. For any
values the exact identity is

    Q_ij=<g_i-g_j,y_j>+phi_j-phi_i.            (21)

The local rows Q_(20,19) and Q_(28,27) are their geometric versions(4)
PLUS nonnegative phi_19 and phi_27. Thus their feasibility still implies
the same norm bounds(14)--(16). The geometric part of Q_(16,27) still
satisfies(20). Consequently every such value repair necessarily pays

    phi_16-phi_27>(9/10)*min(t1,t2).          (22)

This is a quantitative necessary height interface, not a proof that the
repair is feasible or impossible. It is derived jointly above; no
unverified result is imported as a premise. Zero-conjugate-height or
equal-height repairs cannot work. The cost of satisfying(22) together
with all remaining rows and the true initial distance is unresolved.

For the same reset and constant-half geometry, replace the min-kernel by
an arbitrary Euclidean triple and define

    c12=<v_1,v_2>, c13=<v_1,v_3>, c23=<v_2,v_3>.

Without changing the gradient rule(1), the previously failed row is exactly

    D^2*Q_(16,27)
      = b*B_lo*B_hi*c12-A^2*B_hi*c13+b*B_lo(A-B_lo)*t2
        +[A^2(B_lo-A)-A*b*B_lo]*c23+A^3*t3,         (23)

under saturated values. In the min-kernel family, c12=mu and c13=c23=t3;
this recovers(17). Thus an actual next construction must change a concrete
remote covariance balance, pay joint nonsaturated conjugate heights,
move a core return, or reproduce changing internal H2 gradients. It must
also retain every other old/new/optimum row and the true initial cost.
Equations(22)--(23) are interfaces for a new construction, not a generic Gram
oracle offered as the result and not a second extrapolation template.

The first explicit maximal-transition law and its entire arbitrary-norm
min-kernel enlargement are now closed by the same original-row argument.
Do not spend the final diagnostic job fitting that closed family.
One diagnostic job and one undeclared extrapolation-template slot remain
within this launch; they are not new allocations. The second allocated
research round may use a genuinely changed mechanism after re-reading
TASK.md and current shared guidance. No resource renewal is inferred.
